COMMANDS
============================================================
  ER / e / er / energy
    Usage: /ER
    Perm:  player.setprop | player.setprop.others
  GameSpeed / speed
    Usage: /speed <0.1 | 0.2 | 0.5 | 0.75 | 1.0 | 1.5 | 2.0 | 3.0>
    Perm:  player.windy | player.windy.others
  HideUI / hui
    Usage: /HideUI
    Perm:  player.windy | player.windy.others
  NoFog / fog
    Usage: /nofog
    Perm:  player.windy | player.windy.others
  RemoveCensorship / cancer / rc
    Usage: /RemoveCensorship
    Perm:  player.windy | player.windy.others
  account
    Usage: /create <username> [<UID>]
    Usage: /delete <username>
    Usage: /create <username> <password> [<UID>]
    Usage: /resetpass <username> <password>
  achievement / am
    Usage: /(grant|revoke) <achievementId>
    Usage: /progress <achievementId> <progress>
    Usage: /grantall
    Usage: /revokeall
    Perm:  player.achievement | player.achievement.others
  announce / a
    Usage: /<content>
    Usage: /refresh
    Usage: /(tpl|revoke) <templateId>
    Perm:  server.announce
  ban
    Usage: /[<time> [<reason>]]
    Perm:  server.ban
  barrier / br / pb
    Usage: //br [on|off | 1|0]\n\sdescription: removes paimon barrier at will
    Perm:  player.setprop | player.setprop.others
  clear
    Usage: /(all|wp|art|mat) [lv<max level>] [r<max refinement>] [<max rarity>*]
    Perm:  player.clearinv | player.clearinv.others
  coop
    Usage: /[<host UID>]
    Perm:  server.coop | server.coop.others
  cutscene / c
    Usage: /[<cutsceneId>]
    Perm:  player.cutscene | player.cutscene.others
  debug
    Usage: //debug
    Perm:  grasscutter.command.debug
  enter_dungeon / enterdungeon / dungeon
    Usage: /<dungeonId>
    Perm:  player.enterdungeon | player.enterdungeon.others
  entity
    Usage: /<configId gadget> [state<state>] [maxhp<maxhp>] [hp<hp>(0 for infinite)] [atk<atk>] [def<def>]
    Usage: /<configId monster> [ai<aiId>] [maxhp<maxhp>] [hp<hp>(0 for infinite)] [atk<atk>] [def<def>]
    Perm:  server.entity
  fps
    Usage: /fps
    Perm:  player.windy | player.windy.others
  give / g / item / giveitem
    Usage: /(<itemId>|<avatarId>|all|weapons|mats|avatars) [lv<level>] [r<refinement>] [x<amount>] [c<constellation>] [sl<skilllevel>]
    Usage: /<artifactId> [lv<level>] [x<amount>] [<mainPropId>] [<appendPropId>[,<times>]]...
    Perm:  player.give | player.give.others
  group / gr
    Usage: /(refresh) [<groupId>] [<suiteId>]
    Perm:  player.group | player.group.others
  heal / h
    Usage: /heal
    Perm:  player.heal | player.heal.others
  help
    Usage: /[<command>]
  info / troubleshoot / helpme
    Usage: //info
  kick / restart
    Usage: /kick
    Perm:  server.kick
  killCharacter / suicide / kill
    Usage: /killCharacter
    Perm:  player.killcharacter | player.killcharacter.others
  killall
    Usage: /[<sceneId>]
    Perm:  server.killall | server.killall.others
  language / lang
    Usage: /[<language code>]
  list / players
    Usage: /[uid]
  permission
    Usage: /add <permission>
    Usage: /remove <permission>
    Usage: /clear
    Usage: /list
    Perm:  permission
  position / pos
    Usage: /position
  quest / q
    Usage: /(add|finish|running|talking|debug|triggers|grouptriggers) [<questId>]
    Usage: /dungeons
    Perm:  player.quest | player.quest.others
  reload
    Usage: /reload
    Perm:  server.reload
  resetConst / resetconstellation
    Usage: /[all]
    Perm:  player.resetconstellation | player.resetconstellation.others
  resetShopLimit / resetshop
    Usage: /resetShopLimit
    Perm:  server.resetshop | server.resetshop.others
  sendMail
    Usage: /(<userId>|all) [<templateId>]
    Usage: /help
    Perm:  server.sendmail
  sendMessage / say / sendservmsg / sendservermessage / b / broadcast
    Usage: /<message>
    Perm:  server.sendmessage | server.sendmessage.others
  setConst / setconstellation
    Usage: /<constellation level> [all]
    Perm:  player.setconstellation | player.setconstellation.others
  setFetterLevel / setfetterlvl / setfriendship
    Usage: /<level>
    Perm:  player.setfetterlevel | player.setfetterlevel.others
  setProp / prop
    Usage: /<prop> <value>
    Perm:  player.setprop | player.setprop.others
  setSceneTag / tag
    Usage: /<add|remove|unlockall|reset|list> [sceneTagId]
    Perm:  player.setscenetag | player.setscenetag.others
  setStats / stats / stat
    Usage: /[set] <stat> <value>
    Usage: /(lock|freeze) <stat> [<value>]
    Usage: /(unlock|unfreeze) <stat>
    Perm:  player.setstats | player.setstats.others
  sgv / serverglobalvalue
    Usage: /sgv
  sound / audio
    Usage: /[<audioname>] [<x><y><z>]
    Perm:  player.sound | player.sound.others
  spawn / drop / s
    Usage: /<itemId> [x<amount>] [blk<blockId>] [grp<groupId>] [cfg<configId>] [<x> <y> <z>] [<rotX> <rotY> <rotZ>]
    Usage: /<gadgetId> [x<amount>] [state<state>] [maxhp<maxhp>] [hp<hp>(0 for infinite)] [atk<atk>] [def<def>] [blk<blockId>] [grp<groupId>] [cfg<configId>] [<x> <y> <z>] [<rotX> <rotY> <rotZ>]
    Usage: /<monsterId> [x<amount>] [lv<level>] [ai<aiId>] [maxhp<maxhp>] [hp<hp>(0 for infinite)] [atk<atk>] [def<def>] [blk<blockId>] [grp<groupId>] [cfg<configId>] [<x> <y> <z>] [<rotX> <rotY> <rotZ>]
    Perm:  server.spawn | server.spawn.others
  stop / shutdown
    Usage: /stop
    Perm:  server.stop
  switchelement / se
    Usage: /anemo|geo|electro|dendro
  talent
    Usage: /set <talentId> <level>
    Usage: /(n|e|q|all) <level>
    Usage: /getid
    Perm:  player.settalent | player.settalent.others
  team
    Usage: /add <avatarId,...>
    Usage: /(remove|set) [index|first|last|index-index,...]
    Perm:  player.team | player.team.others
  teleport / tp
    Usage: /<x> <y> <z> [sceneId]
    Perm:  player.teleport | player.teleport.others
  teleportAll / tpall
    Usage: /teleportAll
    Perm:  player.tpall | player.tpall.others
  trialAvatarActivity / taa
    Usage: /change <scheduleId>
    Usage: /toggleDungeon <index(start from 1)|all>
    Usage: /toggleReward <index(start from 1)|all>
    Perm:  player.trialavataractivity | player.trialavataractivity.others
  uid
    Usage: /set <what you want>
    Usage: /default
    Perm:  player.windy.others
  unban
    Usage: /unban
    Perm:  server.ban
  unlockall
    Usage: /
    Perm:  player.unlockall | player.unlockall.others
  weather / w
    Usage: /weather [<weatherId>] [<climateType>]

    Usage: /windy
